From the project root, start\\nit in the foreground with:\\n\\n```sh\\nPORT=8080 ./run.sh\\n```\\n\\n`run.sh` compiles `Main.java` and then runs `Main` in the foreground. The\\nserver binds only to `127.0.0.1` and reads its port from `PORT` (defaulting to\\n`8080` when it is not set). With the server running, a minimal verification\\nis:\\n\\n```sh\\ncurl -i http://127.0.0.1:8080/health\\n```\\n\\nThe expected response is HTTP 200 with `{\\\"ok\\\":true}`. To compile without\\nstarting a server, run `javac Main.java`.\\n\\n## Implementation layout\\n\\nThe project deliberately has a small surface:\\n\\n- `run.sh` is the foreground launcher.\\n- `Main.java` is the entry point and contains the HTTP server, centralized\\n  router, domain operations, validation, JSON parsing/encoding helpers, and\\n  SQLite adapter. Its sections are routing, domain actions, persistence, then\\n  JSON and validation utilities; nested records/classes are the domain model.\\n- `game.db` is created beside the source at runtime and is the durable state;\\n  it is not a source artifact.\\n\\n`Main.main` initializes storage, creates `com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer`,\\nbinds it to the loopback address, and sends every request to `handle`.\\n`handle` keeps routing centralized: fixed and parameterized non-POST routes\\nare checked first, then POST-only resource routes and fixed calculation\\nendpoints. It maps domain exceptions to the established JSON status responses.\\nRoute order, exact methods, and the single-read handling of request bodies are\\ncompatibility-sensitive; do not reorder or broaden these checks without\\nendpoint-level tests.\\n\\n## State and persistence\\n\\nThe live state is held in insertion-ordered `LinkedHashMap` caches for users,\\ncombat sessions, monsters, items, campaign-management campaigns, and\\nturn-based play campaigns. Campaign-management campaigns own ordered maps of\\ncharacters, events, quests, factions, NPCs, inventory, crafting projects, and\\nscheduled sessions; combat sessions own initiative order and per-target\\nconditions. Ordering is observable in several response bodies, so it is\\nintentional. A shared cache-clear helper is used by both reload and reset so\\neach replaces the complete in-memory snapshot.\\n\\nAll state-changing domain methods are `synchronized`. After a successful\\nmutation they call `saveStorage`, which replaces a complete transactional\\nSQLite snapshot. `CLEAR_STORAGE_TABLES` defines the child-before-owner deletion\\norder shared by snapshots and resets. On startup, `initializeStorage` creates\\nschema version 1 if needed and `loadStorage` reconstructs the caches. The\\nstorage adapter invokes the platform `sqlite3` executable; no Java database\\ndependency is used. Combat session state is JSON encoded and Base64 stored,\\nwhile the other domains use normal tables. The storage reset endpoint clears\\nboth the database tables and the live caches.\\n\\n## API/domain groupings\\n\\n- Service and storage: `GET /health`, `GET /v1/storage/status`, and\\n  `POST /v1/storage/reset`.\\n- Core rules: dice statistics, ability checks, adjusted encounter XP, and\\n  initiative ordering under `/v1/dice`, `/v1/checks`, `/v1/encounters`, and\\n  `/v1/initiative`.\\n- Character and PHB helpers: ability modifiers, proficiency, derived stats,\\n  spell slots, long rests, and equipment load under `/v1/characters` and\\n  `/v1/phb`.\\n- Combat: session creation plus condition and turn-advance actions under\\n  `/v1/combat/sessions`.\\n- Authentication: registration and login under `/v1/auth`; passwords are\\n  PBKDF2 hashes with random salts, while the returned session token remains\\n  deterministic by contract.\\n- Compendium: creation and lookup of monsters and items under\\n  `/v1/compendium`.\\n- Campaign management and DM tools: campaign creation, characters, events,\\n  quests, factions, NPCs, inventory/equipment, crafting, scheduling, reports,\\n  state, audit, and export under `/v1/campaigns`; encounter building, loot\\n  parcels, and session recaps under `/v1/dm`.\\n- Live play campaigns: authenticated ownership, membership, start,\\n  narration/actions/resolutions, travel and rest turns, turn context and\\n  nudges, and campaign documents under `/v1/play/campaigns`.\\n\\n## Safe extension and testing conventions\\n\\nTreat HTTP status codes, JSON field names/order, validation boundaries,\\ndeterministic tie-breakers, and persistence/restart behavior as public\\ncontracts. Add a route in the matching router section and keep all accepted\\nmethods explicit. Reuse the existing validation helpers (`string`, `integer`,\\n`array`, `requiredText`, and range helpers) so malformed JSON and wrong types\\ncontinue to become the standard 400 response.\\n\\nFor a new persistent field, update schema initialization, snapshot writing,\\nand loading together; preserve ordering with `LinkedHashMap` and explicit\\n`position` columns where collections are serialized. Keep record IDs global\\nwithin their campaign record kind, matching the existing duplicate checks.\\nExercise both the normal request and a restart/readback path. \\\",\\\\\\\"hp_max\\\\\\\":\\\" + hp + \\\",\\\\\\\"armor_class\\\\\\\":\\\" + armorClass + \\\",\\\\\\\"modifiers\\\\\\\":{\\\\\\\"str\\\\\\\":\\\" + str\\n            + \\\",\\\\\\\"dex\\\\\\\":\\\" + dex + \\\",\\\\\\\"con\\\\\\\":\\\" + con + \\\",\\\\\\\"int\\\\\\\":\\\" + intel + \\\",\\\\\\\"wis\\\\\\\":\\\" + wis\\n            + \\\",\\\\\\\"cha\\\\\\\":\\\" + String longRest(Map<String, Object> b) {\\n        long level = characterLevel(b, \\\"level\\\");\\n        long hpCurrent = integer(b, \\\"hp_current\\\");\\n        long hpMax = positive(integer(b, \\\"hp_max\\\"));\\n        long hitDiceSpent = integer(b, \\\"hit_dice_spent\\\");\\n        long exhaustion = integer(b, \\\"exhaustion_level\\\");\\n        if (hpCurrent < 0 || hpCurrent > hpMax monsterCount) {\n        return monsterCount == 1 ? 1 : monsterCount == 2 ? 1.5 : monsterCount <= 6 ? 2\n            : monsterCount <= 10 ? 2.5 : monsterCount <= 14 ? 3 : 4;\n    }\n\n    private static String recommendation(String difficulty) {\n        return switch (difficulty) {\n            case \"trivial\" -> \"no meaningful risk\";\n            case \"easy\" -> \"safe warm-up\";\n            case \"medium\" -> \"balanced challenge\";\n            case \"hard\" -> \"dangerous fight\";\n            default -> \"consider reducing foes\";\n        };\n    }\n\n    private static long[] hashed.\n    private static byte[] hashPassword(String password) {\n        byte[] salt = new byte[16];\n        RANDOM.nextBytes(salt);\n        byte[] digest = pbkdf2(password.toCharArray(), salt);\n        byte[] stored = new byte[salt.length + digest.length];\n        System.arraycopy(salt, 0, stored, 0, salt.length);\n        System.arraycopy(digest, 0, stored, salt.length, digest.length);\n        return stored;\n    }\n\n    private static boolean verifyPassword(String password, byte[] stored) {\n        if (stored.length < 17) return false;\n        byte[] salt = new byte[16];\n        System.arraycopy(stored, 0, salt, 0, salt.length);\n        byte[] expected = new byte[stored.length - salt.length];\n        System.arraycopy(stored, salt.length, expected, 0, expected.length);\n        return MessageDigest.isEqual(expected, pbkdf2(password.toCharArray(), salt));\n    }\n\n    private static byte[] pbkdf2(char[] password, byte[] salt) {\n        PBEKeySpec private static String storageState(CombatSession session) {\n        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder(\"{\\\"id\\\":\\\"\").append(escape(session.id)).append(\"\\\",\\\"round\\\":\").append(session.round)\n            .append(\",\\\"turn_index\\\":\").append(session.turnIndex).append(\",\\\"order\\\":[\");\n        for (int i = 0; i < session.order.size(); i++) {\n            if (i > 0) out.append(',');\n out.append(\"{\\\"name\\\":\\\"\").append(escape(c.name)).append(\"\\\",\\\"dex\\\":\").append(c.dex).append(\",\\\"score\\\":\").append(c.score).append('}');\n        }\n        out.append(\"],\\\"conditions\\\":{\");\n        boolean first = true;\n        for (Combatant c : session.order) {\n            if (!first) out.append(',');\n            first = false;\n            out.append('\"').append(escape(c.name)).append(\"\\\":\").append(conditionsArray(session.conditions.get(c.name)));\n        }\n        return out.append(\"}}\").toString();\n    }\n\n    private static void restoreSession(String state) {\n        Map<String, Object> saved = object(state);\n        List<Combatant> order = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Object item : array(saved, \"order\")) {\n            Map<String, Object> c = asObject(item);\n            order.add(new Combatant(string(c, \"name\"), integer(c, \"dex\"), integer(c, \"score\")));\n        }\n        Map<String, List<Condition>> conditions = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n        Map<String, Object> savedConditions = asObject(saved.get(\"conditions\"));\n        for (Combatant c : order) {\n            List<Condition> attached = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (Object item : array(savedConditions, c.name)) {\n                Map<String, Object> condition = asObject(item);\n                attached.add(new Condition(string(condition, \"condition\"), integer(condition, \"remaining_rounds\")));\n            }\n            conditions.put(c.name, attached);\n        }\n        CombatSession session = new CombatSession(string(saved, \"id\"), order, conditions);\n        session.round = integer(saved, \"round\");\n        session.turnIndex = Math.toIntExact(integer(saved, \"turn_index\"));\n        SESSIONS.put(session.id, session);\n    }\n\n    private static String sqlText(String value) { return \"'\" + value.replace(\"'\", \"''\") + \"'\"; }\n    private static String sql(String statement) throws IOException { return sql(statement, false); }\n    private static String sql(String statement, boolean output) throws IOException {\n        Process process = new ProcessBuilder(\"sqlite3\", \"-batch\", \"-noheader\", DATABASE.toString(), statement).start();\n        String stdout = new String(process.getInputStream().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        String stderr = new String(process.getErrorStream().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        try { if (process.waitFor() != 0) throw new IOException(\"sqlite error: \" + stderr); }\n        catch (InterruptedException e) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); throw new IOException(\"sqlite interrupted\", e); }\n        return output ? stdout : \"\";\n    }\n\n    private static CombatSession session(String id) {\n        CombatSession session = SESSIONS.get(id);\n        if (session == null) throw new UnknownSession();\n        return session;\n    }\n\n    private static String sessionId(String path, String suffix) {\n        return path.substring(\"/v1/combat/sessions/\".length(), path.length() - suffix.length());\n    }\n\n    private static String sessionState(CombatSession session, boolean includeConditions) {\n        Combatant active = session.order.get(session.turnIndex);\n        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder(\"{\\\"id\\\":\\\"\").append(escape(session.id)).append(\"\\\",\\\"round\\\":\")\n            .append(session.round).append(\",\\\"turn_index\\\":\").append(session.turnIndex).append(\",\\\"active\\\":\")\n            .append(combatantJson(active));\n        if (!includeConditions) {\n            out.append(\",\\\"order\\\":[\");\n            for (int i = 0; i < session.order.size(); i++) { if (i > 0) out.append(','); out.append(combatantJson(session.order.get(i))); }\n            return out.append(\"]}\").toString();\n        }\n        out.append(\",\\\"conditions\\\":{\");\n        boolean first = true;\n        for (Combatant combatant : session.order) {\n            List<Condition> attached = session.conditions.get(combatant.name);\n            if (!first) out.append(',');\n            first = false;\n            out.append(\"\\\"\").append(escape(combatant.name)).append(\"\\\":\").append(conditionsArray(attached));\n        }\n        return out.append(\"}}\").toString();\n    }\n\n    private static String combatantJson(Combatant c) { return \"{\\\"name\\\":\\\"\" + escape(c.name) + \"\\\",\\\"score\\\":\" + c.score + \"}\"; }\n    private static String conditionsArray(List<Condition> conditions) {\n        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder(\"[\");\n        for (int i = 0; i < conditions.size(); i++) {\n            if (i > 0) out.append(',');\n            Condition condition = conditions.get(i);\n            out.append(\"{\\\"condition\\\":\\\"\").append(escape(condition.condition)).append(\"\\\",\\\"remaining_rounds\\\":\").append(condition.remainingRounds).append('}');\n        }\n        return out.append(']').toString();\n    }\n\n    private static String abilityModifier(Map<String, Object> b) {\n        long score = abilityScore(b, \"score\");\n        return \"{\\\"score\\\":\" + score + \",\\\"modifier\\\":\" + modifier(score) + \"}\";\n    }\n\n    private static String proficiency(Map<String, Object> b) {\n        long level = characterLevel(b, \"level\");\n        return \"{\\\"level\\\":\" + level + \",\\\"proficiency_bonus\\\":\" + proficiencyBonus(level) + \"}\";\n    }\n\n    private static String derivedStats(Map<String, Object> b) {\n
